    "outfit_suggestion": "**Outfit 1: Y2K Streetwear**\n*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n*   **Bottoms:** Baggy straight-leg jeans, dark wash (w_001)\n*   **Shoes:** Chunky white sneakers (w_007)\n*   **Suggested Addition:** A pink shoulder bag or tinted sunglasses\n\n**Why it works:** The fitted, cropped silhouette of the baby tee balances the volume of the high-waisted, baggy dark-wash jeans for a classic Y2K proportion play. The chunky white sneakers tie the casual streetwear aesthetic together.\n\n***\n\n**Outfit 2: Vintage Edge**\n*   **Top:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print\n*   **Outerwear:** Vintage black denim jacket (w_006)\n*   **Bottoms:** Wide-leg khaki trousers (w_002)\n*   **Shoes:** Black combat boots (w_008)\n*   **Accessories:** Black crossbody bag (w_010)\n\n**Why it works:** Pairing the sweet, pink-and-purple butterfly graphic tee with the slightly cropped black denim jacket and rugged combat boots creates a cool contrast between girly and grunge styles, while the wide-leg khaki trousers ground the look with neutral earth tones.",

    "outfit_suggestion": "**Outfit 1: Y2K Streetwear**\n* **Pieces:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print, Baggy straight-leg jeans (dark wash), Chunky white sneakers, Black crossbody bag\n* **Why it works:** The fitted, cropped baby tee balances the high-waisted, baggy fit of the dark wash jeans for a classic early 2000s silhouette. The white sneakers tie in the tee's white base, and the crossbody bag keeps it practical and styled. (You could add a beaded necklace or butterfly clips to complete the Y2K aesthetic).\n\n**Outfit 2: Vintage Casual**\n* **Pieces:** Y2K Baby Tee — Butterfly Print, Vintage black denim jacket, Wide-leg khaki trousers, Black combat boots\n* **Why it works:** Pairing the pastel butterfly graphic tee with earth-toned khaki trousers creates an effortless contrast. Layering the slightly cropped black denim jacket and grounding the look with black combat boots adds a touch of grunge to balance the sweet graphic.",
